Simple—A generalized trade area based on the percentages of customers corresponding to each store will be generated. Amoeba—Points representing the boundary of the polygon trade area will be connected using natural curvature. Detailed—Points representing the boundary of the polygon trade area will be connected using straight lines. Detailed with smoothing—Points representing the boundary of the polygon trade area will be connected with smoothed curves using cubic splines. This method takes into account the shape and pattern of the customer distributions. This is the default. Threshold Rings—Concentric rings that expand from input stores until they contain the specified threshold of customers will be generated. Threshold Drive Times—Polygons that expand from stores along network routes until they contain the specified threshold of customers will be generated."}, {"parameter": "rings", "label_zh": "Radii (%)", "label_en": "Radii (%)", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The values that will be used to represent the percentage of customers, for example, total count or a customer attribute and total sales assigned to each store. Each value represents one trade area polygon."}, {"parameter": "customer_aggregation_type", "label_zh": "Customer Aggregation Type", "label_en": "Customer Aggregation Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of aggregation that will be used. Count—Percentage-based trade areas will be calculated using the geographic locations of customers. This is the default. Weight—Percentage-based trade areas will be calculated using a customer attribute, for example, sales."}, {"parameter": "customer_weight_field", "label_zh": "Customer Weight Field （可選）", "label_en": "Customer Weight Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that will be used to calculate the trade areas. This is based on either the number of customers (count) or the calculated weighted value assigned to each customer."}, {"parameter": "exclude_outlying_customers", "label_zh": "Exclude Outlying Customers （可選）", "label_en": "Exclude Outlying Customers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether outlying customers will be excluded from the trade area generation. Checked—Outlying customers will be excluded. Unchecked—Outlying customers will not be excluded; all customers will be considered. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "cutoff_distance", "label_zh": "Cut-off Distance （可選）", "label_en": "Cut-off Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance beyond which customers will be considered outliers and excluded from consideration during trade area generation."}, {"parameter": "dissolve_option", "label_zh": "Dissolve Option （可選）", "label_en": "Dissolve Option (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether polygons of the entire area will be created or the polygons will be split into individual features. When the Method parameter is set to Threshold Drive Times, the only available option is Overlap. Overlap—Output polygons will be generated in which each feature begins at zero and grows to satisfy the specified percentage of customers. For example, if you specify a trade area of 50 percent and 70 percent of customers, one polygon will be generated to include 0 to 50 percent and a second polygon will include all 0 to 70 percent of customers. This is the default. Split—Output polygons will be generated for individual features based on the specified percentage breaks. For example, if you specify a trade area of 50 percent and 70 percent of customers, one polygon will be generated to include 0 to 50 percent and a second polygon will include 50 to 70 percent of customers."}, {"parameter": "use_customer_centroids", "label_zh": "Use Customers Centroid for Trade Area Center （可選）", "label_en": "Use Customers Centroid for Trade Area Center (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the centroid of your customer area will be used to calculate trade areas outward from this point. Checked—The centroid of customer points will be used to calculate trade areas. Unchecked—The centroid of customer points will not used; store location will be used as the starting point to calculate trade areas. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "distance_type", "label_zh": "Distance Type （可選）", "label_en": "Distance Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The method of travel that will be used to calculate the distance."}, {"parameter": "units", "label_zh": "Distance Units （可選）", "label_en": "Distance Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The units that will be used for the distance values."}, {"parameter": "travel_direction", "label_zh": "Travel Direction （可選）", "label_en": "Travel Direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the direction of travel toward or away from stores. Toward stores—The direction of travel will be toward stores. This is the default. Away from stores—The direction of travel will be away from stores."}, {"parameter": "time_of_day", "label_zh": "Time of Day （可選）", "label_en": "Time of Day (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The time and date that will be used when calculating distance."}, {"parameter": "time_zone", "label_zh": "Time Zone （可選）", "label_en": "Time Zone (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the time zone that will be used for the Time of Day parameter. Time Zone at Location—The time zone in which the territories are located will be used. This is the default. UTC—Coordinated universal time (UTC) will be used."}, {"parameter": "search_tolerance", "label_zh": "Search Tolerance （可選）", "label_en": "Search Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The maximum distance that input points can be from the network. Points located beyond the search tolerance will be excluded from processing. This parameter requires a distance value and units for the tolerance."}, {"parameter": "polygon_detail", "label_zh": "Polygon Detail （可選）", "label_en": "Polygon Detail (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the level of detail that will be used for the output drive time polygons. Standard—Polygons with a standard level of detail will be created. This is the default. Generalized—Generalized polygons will be created using the hierarchy present in the network data source to produce results quickly. High—Polygons with a high level of detail will be created for applications in which precise results are important."}, {"parameter": "iterations_limit", "label_zh": "Iterations Limit （可選）", "label_en": "Iterations Limit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "Restricts the number of drive times that can be used to find the optimal threshold limit."}, {"parameter": "minimum_step", "label_zh": "Minimum Step （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Step (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The minimum increment distance or time—for example, 1 mile or 1 minute—that will be used between iterations to expand until the threshold is reached."}, {"parameter": "target_percent_diff", "label_zh": "Threshold Percent Difference （可選）", "label_en": "Threshold Percent Difference (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The maximum percentage difference between the target value and threshold value that will be used when determining the threshold drive time, for example, 5 percent. The default value is 5."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.ba.CustomerDerivedTA(in_stores_layer, store_id_field, in_customers_layer, link_field, out_feature_class, method, rings, customer_aggregation_type, {customer_weight_field}, {exclude_outlying_customers}, {cutoff_distance}, {dissolve_option}, {use_customer_centroids}, {distance_type}, {units}, {travel_direction}, {time_of_day}, {time_zone}, {search_tolerance}, {polygon_detail}, {iterations_limit}, {minimum_step}, {target_percent_diff})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_stores_layer、store_id_field、in_customers_layer、link_field、out_feature_class、method、rings、customer_aggregation_type、customer_weight_field、exclude_outlying_customers、cutoff_distance、dissolve_option、use_customer_centroids、distance_type、units、travel_direction、time_of_day、time_zone、search_tolerance、polygon_detail、iterations_limit、minimum_step、target_percent_diff。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 045028：地統計圖層或 XML 文件不能表示支持的模型。 · 描述

**id**　505e468352627494c0-1

**english**　045028: The geostatistical layer or XML file does not represent a supported model.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 45001-45100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45026-45050-045028.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45026-45050-045028.html

**text**　某些地統計地理處理工具需要使用地統計向導中提供的克里金法之一創建的輸入地統計圖層或 XML 文件。 指定的地統計圖層不是使用這些克里金法之一生成的。
經驗貝葉斯克里金、3D 經驗貝葉斯克里金、EBK 回歸預測和協同克里金模型不能用于此工具。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45026-45050-045028.html#description

## 110501：無法計算該索引，因為一個或多個變量具有負值。 · 描述

**id**　5065c2747c1d2d0f95-1

**english**　110501: The index could not be calculated because one or more variables have negative values.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110501-110600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110501.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110501.html

**text**　如果為合并比例變量的方法參數指定了乘或幾何平均數選項，則任何比例變量都不能包含負值。
此錯誤可能在各種情況下發生，具體取決于縮放輸入變量的方法參數的值：
原始值 - 輸入欄位的任何值都可能包含負值。
Z 得分 - 輸入欄位的任何值都可能具有負的 z 得分，具體取決于輸入欄位的平均值和標準差。
Z 得分（自定義）- 輸入欄位的任何值都可能具有負的 z 得分，具體取決于自定義平均值和標準差。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110501-110525-110501.html#description

**id**　50661c2f21304e39e9-3

**english**　Add Expand Container By Category Rule

**chapter**

**text**　該工具會基于輸入邏輯示意圖模板影響任何現有邏輯示意圖的一致性。 所有現有邏輯示意圖都將變得不一致，并會在打開時顯示一致性警告圖標 ，直至對其進行更新。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 添加按類別展開容器規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 用法

**id**　50661c2f21304e39e9-4

**english**　Add Expand Container By Category Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html

**text**　系統在使用 utility network 服務時不支持此工具。 必須使用文件或移動地理資料庫中的公共設施網絡或企業級地理資料庫中公共設施網絡的資料庫連接。 當使用企業級地理資料庫時，需要滿足以下要求： 輸入網絡參數值必須來自作為 資料庫公共設施網絡所有者 建立的資料庫連接。 連接的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶賬戶必須為 門戶公共設施網絡所有者。 該工具必須連接到預期為公開且不受保護的默認版本。
“展開容器”規則可用于展開邏輯示意圖，方法是添加符合以下要求的內容圖徵或對象： 它們包含在邏輯示意圖輸入的一個容器中。 它們同邏輯示意圖輸入中的內容共享同一容器。
展開容器規則可展開邏輯示意圖中表示的特定網絡容器圖徵或對象。 此工具允許按網絡類別配置要運行的“展開容器”規則。 這意味著您可以根據是否標記了特定的網絡類別來配置生成邏輯示意圖中網絡容器的展開。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html#usage

**parameter**　container_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 容器類型
- 指定規則將進行處理的容器圖徵的幾何類型。 僅交匯點—展開容器規則僅處理交匯點對交匯點對象容器。 僅邊—展開容器規則僅處理邊對邊對象容器。 僅會處理線性容器 交匯點和邊—展開容器規則將處理任何類型的容器圖徵和對象。 交匯點和邊類型均被處理。 這是默認設置
- String

- 指明如何處理標記為指定網絡類別的容器。 排除類別—將不會展開標記為指定網絡類別的容器，但將展開其他容器。 這是默認設置。 包含類別—展開標記為指定網絡類別的容器。

## 添加按類別展開容器規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 參數

**id**　50661c2f21304e39e9-12

**english**　Add Expand Container By Category Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html

**parameter**　category

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 類別
- 根據規則實施參數值，將排除或包含的網絡類別。 如果規則實施參數設置為包含類別，則必須指定一個或多個網絡類別。 標記為指定類別的所有容器將會在生成邏輯示意圖中展開。 如果將規則實施參數設置為排除類別，則在生成的邏輯示意圖中將不展開與任何標記了指定類別的容器相關的內容，而展開與未標記指定類別的容器相關的內容。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-expand-container-by-category-rule.html#parameters
